          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Installation contracts.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may enter into an installment payment contract for the purchase and installation of energy conservation measures. The contract must provide for payments of not less than 1/15 of the price to be paid within two years from the date of the first operation, and the remaining costs to be paid monthly, not to exceed a 15-year term from the date of the first operation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.65.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Contract continuance.</span></h2>
          <p>Guaranteed energy-savings contracts may extend beyond the fiscal year in which they become effective. The district must include in its annual appropriations measure for each later fiscal year any amounts payable under guaranteed energy-savings contracts during the year. Failure of a board to make such an appropriation does not affect the validity of the guaranteed energy-savings contract or the district's obligations under the contracts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.65.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Public information.</span></h2>
          <p>A guaranteed energy-savings contract must provide that all work plans and other information prepared by the qualified provider in relation to the project, including a detailed description of the project, are public data after the contract is entered into. Information defined as trade secret information under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.37#stat.13.37.1">13.37, subdivision 1</a>, shall remain nonpublic data.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.65.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/263/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 263 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.7.3.0">1992 c 499 art 7 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.5.27.0">1993 c 224 art 5 s 27</a>-29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/647/#laws.5.6.0">1994 c 647 art 5 s 6</a>-12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/224/#laws.0.67.0">1995 c 224 s 67</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.101.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 101</a>-107,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.1.23.0">1998 c 398 art 1 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/4/#laws.6.19.0">1Sp2001 c 4 art 6 s 19</a>-21; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/136/#laws.3.1.0">2007 c 136 art 3 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.651">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.651 ENERGY USE REDUCTION AND REPORTING FOR PUBLIC SCHOOLS.</h1>
        <p>Beginning October 1, 2019, each public school or school district reporting on behalf of a public school must enter and maintain monthly utility consumption data into the Minnesota B3 benchmarking program for all buildings under its custodial control. Reporting by a third party, including automatic reporting by an electric or gas utility, may be used to meet this requirement. A school or school district must not be penalized for failure to comply with this section.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.651.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.6.4.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 6 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.123B.66"><b>123B.66</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.4.29.0">1999 c 241 art 4 s 29</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.662">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.662 AIR VENTILATION PILOT PROGRAM; DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.663">123B.663</a>, the terms in this section have the meanings given unless the language or context clearly indicates that a different meaning is intended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">ANSI.</span></h2>
          <p>"ANSI" means American National Standards Institute.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">ASHRAE.</span></h2>
          <p>"ASHRAE" means American Society of Heating Refrigeration Air Conditioning Engineers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of commerce or the commissioner's representative.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Eligible entity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Eligible entity" means a public school board operating within the state of Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">HVAC.</span></h2>
          <p>"HVAC" means heating, ventilation, and air conditioning.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Licensed professional engineer.</span></h2>
          <p>"Licensed professional engineer" means a professional engineer who holds an active license issued under chapter 326, and is in good standing with and not subject to any disciplinary or other actions by the Board of Architecture, Engineering, Land Surveying, Landscape Architecture, Geoscience, and Interior Design.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">MERV.</span></h2>
          <p>"MERV" means minimum efficiency reporting value as established by ASHRAE Standard 52.2-2017 Method of Testing General Ventilation Air-Cleaning Devices for Removal Efficiency by Particle Size.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Program" means the air ventilation program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Registered apprenticeship program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Registered apprenticeship program" means an apprenticeship program that is registered under chapter 178 or Code of Federal Regulations, title 29, part 29.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.662.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Skilled and trained workforce.</span></h2>
          <p>"Skilled and trained workforce" means a workforce that is paid the prevailing wage rate, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.42">177.42, subdivision </a>6, for the work, and of which at least 80 percent of the construction workers are either registered in or graduates of a registered apprenticeship program for the applicable occupation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.662.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/60/#laws.12.7.0">2023 c 60 art 12 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.663">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.663 AIR VENTILATION PILOT PROGRAM; GRANTS AND GUIDELINES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Grant program establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The Department of Commerce must establish and administer an air ventilation program to award grants to eligible entities under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Air ventilation program account; appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An air ventilation program account is created in the special revenue fund of the state treasury. The commissioner must credit to the account appropriations and transfers made to the account. Earnings, such as interest, dividends, and any other earnings arising from assets of the account, must be credited to the account. Money remaining in the account at the end of a fiscal year does not cancel to the general fund but remains available until expended. The commissioner is the fiscal agent and must manage the account.</p>
          <p>(b) Money in the account is appropriated to the commissioner to pay for grants issued under the program and the reasonable costs incurred by the commissioner to administer the program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Grant awards; priorities; maximums.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may award grants under the program for the following activities:</p>
          <p>(1) completing a heating, ventilation, and air conditioning assessment report;</p>
          <p>(2) HVAC testing, adjusting, and balancing work;</p>
          <p>(3) ventilation equipment upgrades, replacements, or other measures recommended by a heating, ventilation, and air conditioning assessment report;</p>
          <p>(4) work on an HVAC system to improve health, safety, energy, or system efficiency, or to reduce greenhouse gas emissions from the system; and</p>
          <p>(5) other HVAC projects that have not already been approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.595">123B.595</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must prioritize grants that give direct support to schools and school children in communities with high rates of poverty as determined by receipt of federal Title I funding.</p>
          <p>(c) A grant under the program may be used to reimburse an eligible entity for no more than 50 percent of its costs for work described in paragraph (a) and must not exceed a total of $50,000 per school.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Administration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must:</p>
          <p>(1) adopt guidelines for the air ventilation program no later than October 1, 2023;</p>
          <p>(2) establish the timing of grant funding;</p>
          <p>(3) ensure that the program is operating and accepting applications for grants by March 31, 2024; and</p>
          <p>(4) provide technical assistance to eligible entities.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may modify the technical and reporting requirements of the program as necessary to comply with current COVID-19 guidance or any other applicable guidance to achieve the intent of the program and to ensure consistency with related requirements and codes.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Application process.</span></h2>
          <p>An eligible entity must apply to the commissioner for a grant on behalf of a school on a form prescribed by the commissioner. The form must include, at a minimum, the following information:</p>
          <p>(1) a plan to complete a heating, ventilation, and air conditioning assessment report by a skilled and trained workforce; and</p>
          <p>(2) an estimate of total project costs and funding needed to conduct the assessment and subsequent work.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Payment conditions.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may reimburse expenses incurred by the eligible entity while under contract with the department upon receipt of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) a report, verified by a licensed professional engineer, that includes costs of adjustments or repairs necessary to meet minimum ventilation and filtration requirements and that determines whether any cost-effective energy efficiency or electrification upgrades or replacements are warranted or recommended;</p>
          <p>(2) an HVAC verification report that includes the name and address of the school facility and individual or contractor preparing and certifying the report and a description of the assessment, maintenance, adjustment, repair, upgrade, and replacement activities and outcomes; and</p>
          <p>(3) verification that the eligible entity has complied with all requirements. Verification must include:</p>
          <p>(i) documentation that either MERV 13 filters have been installed or verification that the maximum MERV-rated filter that the system is able to effectively handle has been installed;</p>
          <p>(ii) documentation of the MERV rating;</p>
          <p>(iii) the verified ventilation rates for occupied areas of the school and whether those rates meet the requirements set forth in ANSI/ASHRAE Standard 62.1, with an accompanying explanation for any ventilation rates that do not meet applicable requirements documenting why the current system is unable to meet requirements;</p>
          <p>(iv) the verified exhaust for occupied areas and whether those rates meet the requirements set forth in the system design intent;</p>
          <p>(v) documentation of system deficiencies;</p>
          <p>(vi) recommendations for additional maintenance, replacement, or upgrades to improve energy efficiency, safety, or performance, or reduce greenhouse gas emissions;</p>
          <p>(vii) documentation of initial operating verifications, adjustments, and final operating verifications;</p>
          <p>(viii) documentation of any adjustments or repairs performed;</p>
          <p>(ix) verification of carbon dioxide monitors, if required, including correct installation and operation according to regulations;</p>
          <p>(x) make and model of monitors;</p>
          <p>(xi) verification of the contractor's name; and</p>
          <p>(xii) verification that all construction work has been performed by a skilled and trained workforce.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Use of federal funds.</span></h2>
          <p>An eligible entity may utilize available matching funds from federal programs in conjunction with a grant awarded under this section to increase funding amounts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">HVAC report.</span></h2>
          <p>An eligible entity that receives a grant under the program must maintain a copy of the HVAC verification report described in subdivision 6, clause (2), and must make the report available to students, parents, school personnel, and any member of the public upon request.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.663.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Prevailing wage.</span></h2>
          <p>All work for which reimbursement is sought through a grant under the program that is performed after conducting a heating, ventilation, and air conditioning assessment must be performed by a skilled and trained workforce. Any project awarded a grant under the program is subject to the requirements and enforcement provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.27">177.27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.30">177.30</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.32">177.32</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.41">177.41</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.435">177.435</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.44">177.44</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.45">177.45</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.663.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/60/#laws.12.8.0">2023 c 60 art 12 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.67">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.67 SCHOOL BUILDING ACCESSIBILITY CAPITAL IMPROVEMENT GRANT ACT.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.67">123B.67</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.69">123B.69</a> may be cited as the "School Building Accessibility Capital Improvement Grant Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.67.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/373/#laws.0.20.0">1993 c 373 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.164.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 164</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.4.29.0">1999 c 241 art 4 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/464/#laws.3.9.0">2000 c 464 art 3 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.5.19.0">2000 c 489 art 5 s 19</a>,27</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.68">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.68 APPROVAL; APPLICATION FORMS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.68.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Approval by commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of education may approve or disapprove applications under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.69">123B.69</a>. The grant money must be used only to remove architectural barriers from a building or site.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.68.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application forms.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prepare application forms and establish application dates.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.68.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Match.</span></h2>
          <p>A district applying for a grant under this section must match the grant with local district funds.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.68.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/373/#laws.0.21.0">1993 c 373 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.161.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 161</a>,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.4.29.0">1999 c 241 art 4 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/464/#laws.3.9.0">2000 c 464 art 3 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.5.19.0">2000 c 489 art 5 s 19</a>,27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.69">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.69 GRANT APPLICATION PROCESS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.69.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Qualification.</span></h2>
          <p>A school district that meets the criteria required under subdivision 2 may apply for a grant in an amount up to 50 percent of the approved costs of removing architectural barriers from a building or site. A grant may not exceed $150,000 to a recipient district in any fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.69.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Project review.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner, in consultation with the Minnesota State Council on Disability, shall review applications for grants. A school district must apply by July 1 of each year in order to be considered for a grant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.69.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Award of grants.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall examine and consider all applications for grants, and if a district is found not qualified, the commissioner shall promptly notify the district board. The commissioner shall give first priority to districts that have entered into the cooperation and combination process under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.35">123A.35</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.43">123A.43</a>, or that have consolidated since January 1, 1987. The commissioner shall further prioritize grants on the basis of the following: the district's tax burden, the long-term feasibility of the project, the suitability of the project, and the district's need for the project. If the total amount of the applications exceeds the amount that is or can be made available, the commissioner shall award grants according to the commissioner's judgment and discretion and based upon a ranking of the projects according to the factors listed in this paragraph. The commissioner shall promptly certify to each district the amount, if any, of the grant awarded to it.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.69.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Matching revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon being awarded a grant under subdivision 3, the board shall determine the need for additional revenue. If the board determines that the local match cannot be made from existing revenue, the board may levy according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.58">123B.58</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.69.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Project budget.</span></h2>
          <p>A district that receives a grant must provide the commissioner with the project budget and any other information the commissioner requests.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.69.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/373/#laws.0.22.0">1993 c 373 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/463/#laws.0.38.0">1996 c 463 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.162.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 162</a>,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.4.29.0">1999 c 241 art 4 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/464/#laws.3.9.0">2000 c 464 art 3 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.5.19.0">2000 c 489 art 5 s 19</a>,27</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.70">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.70 SCHOOL DISTRICT CONSTRUCTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.70.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Commissioner approval.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) In determining whether to give a school facility a positive, negative, or unfavorable review and comment, the commissioner must evaluate the proposals for facilities using the information provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71#stat.123B.71.9">123B.71, subdivision 9</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) In the case of a proposal for a new school, the local school board retains the authority to determine the minimum acreage needed to accommodate the school and related facilities. The commissioner may evaluate the proposals but must not issue a negative or unfavorable review and comment under this section for a school facility solely based on too little acreage of the proposed school site.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) In the case of a proposal to renovate an existing school, the local school board retains the authority to determine whether to renovate an existing school or to build a new school regardless of the acreage of the current school site or the cost of the renovation relative to the cost of building a new school. The commissioner's evaluation of whether to replace a facility must not be solely based upon the ratio of renovation costs to replacement costs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.70.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Positive review and comment.</span></h2>
          <p>If the commissioner submits a positive review and comment for a proposal according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71">123B.71</a>, the school board may proceed with the construction according to the requirements of applicable laws.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.70.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Negative review and comment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the commissioner submits a negative review and comment for a proposal according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71">123B.71</a>, the following steps must be taken:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner must notify the school board of the proposed negative review and comment and schedule a public meeting within 60 days of the notification within that school district to discuss the proposed negative review and comment on the school facility; and</p>
          <p>(2) the school board shall appoint an advisory task force of up to five members to advise the school board and the commissioner on the advantages, disadvantages, and alternatives to the proposed facility at the public meeting. One member of the advisory task force must also be a member of the county facilities group.</p>
          <p>(b) After attending the public meeting, the commissioner shall reconsider the proposal. If the commissioner submits a negative review and comment, the school board may appeal that decision under chapter 14.</p>
          <p>(c) A school board may not proceed with construction if the commissioner's negative review and comment is upheld or if the commissioner's negative review and comment is not appealed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.70.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Unfavorable review and comment.</span></h2>
          <p>If the commissioner submits an unfavorable review and comment for a proposal under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71">123B.71</a>, the school board, by resolution of the board, must reconsider construction. If, upon reconsideration, the school board decides to proceed with construction, it may initiate proceedings for issuing bonds to finance construction under chapter 475. Unless 60 percent of the voters at the election approve of issuing the obligations, the board is not authorized to issue the obligations.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.70.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/718/#laws.8.1.0">1988 c 718 art 8 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.5.1.0">1990 c 562 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.5.1.0">1991 c 265 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.5.1.0">1992 c 499 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/399/#laws.2.2.0">1996 c 399 art 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.1.58.0">1998 c 397 art 1 s 58</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.6.3.0">1998 c 398 art 6 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/254/#laws.0.11.0">2000 c 254 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.4.6.0">2009 c 96 art 4 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.71">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.71 REVIEW AND COMMENT FOR SCHOOL DISTRICT CONSTRUCTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.18.24.0">2014 c 312 art 18 s 24</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Project.</span></h2>
          <p>The construction, remodeling, or improvement of a building or site of an educational facility at an estimated cost exceeding $100,000 is a project under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.42#stat.177.42.2">177.42, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.4.28.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 4 s 28</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.18.24.0">2014 c 312 art 18 s 24</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Final plans.</span></h2>
          <p>If a construction contract has not been awarded within two years of approval, the approval shall not be valid. After approval, final plans and the approval shall be made available, if requested, to the commissioner of education. If substantial changes are made to the initial approved plans, documents reflecting the changes shall be submitted to the commissioner for approval. Upon completing a project, the school board shall certify to the commissioner that the project was completed according to the approved plans.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Condemnation of school buildings.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may condemn school buildings and sites determined to be unfit or unsafe for that use.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Rulemaking.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of education may adopt rules for public school buildings.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Review and comment.</span></h2>
          <p>A school district, a special education cooperative, or a cooperative unit of government, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24#stat.123A.24.2">123A.24, subdivision 2</a>, must not enter into an installment contract for purchase or a lease agreement, hold a referendum for bonds, nor solicit bids for new construction, expansion, or remodeling of an educational facility that requires an expenditure in excess of $500,000 per school site if it has a capital loan outstanding, or $2,000,000 per school site if it does not have a capital loan outstanding, prior to review and comment by the commissioner. New construction, expansion, or remodeling of an educational facility funded only with general education revenue, lease levy proceeds from an additional capital expenditure levy under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.40#stat.126C.40.1">126C.40, subdivision 1</a>, capital facilities bond proceeds, or long-term facilities maintenance revenue is exempt from this provision. A capital project under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.63">123B.63</a> addressing only technology is exempt from this provision if the district submits a school board resolution stating that funds approved by the voters will be used only as authorized in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.14">126C.10, subdivision 14</a>. A school board shall not separate portions of a single project into components to avoid the requirements of this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Information required.</span></h2>
          <p>A school board proposing to construct, expand, or remodel a facility that requires a review and comment under subdivision 8 shall submit to the commissioner a proposal containing information including at least the following:</p>
          <p>(1) the geographic area and population to be served, preschool through grade 12 student enrollments for the past five years, and student enrollment projections for the next five years;</p>
          <p>(2) a list of existing facilities by year constructed, their uses, and an assessment of the extent to which alternate facilities are available within the school district boundaries and in adjacent school districts;</p>
          <p>(3) a list of the specific deficiencies of the facility that demonstrate the need for a new or renovated facility to be provided, the process used to determine the deficiencies, a list of those deficiencies that will and will not be addressed by the proposed project, and a list of the specific benefits that the new or renovated facility will provide to the students, teachers, and community users served by the facility;</p>
          <p>(4) a description of the project, including the specification of site and outdoor space acreage and square footage allocations for classrooms, laboratories, and support spaces; estimated expenditures for the major portions of the project; and the dates the project will begin and be completed;</p>
          <p>(5) a description of the project's plans for gender-neutral single-user restrooms, locker room privacy stalls, or other spaces with privacy features, including single-user shower stalls, changing stalls, or other single-user facilities;</p>
          <p>(6) a specification of the source of financing the project, including applicable statutory citations; the scheduled date for a bond issue or school board action; a schedule of payments, including debt service equalization aid; and the effect of a bond issue on local property taxes by the property class and valuation; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) documents obligating the school district and contractors to comply with items (i) to (vii) in planning and executing the project:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.345">471.345</a> governing municipal contracts;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) sustainable design;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) school facility commissioning under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.72">123B.72</a> certifying the plans and designs for the heating, ventilating, air conditioning, and air filtration for an extensively renovated or new facility meet or exceed current code standards, including the ASHRAE air filtration standard 52.1;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) American National Standards Institute Acoustical Performance Criteria, Design Requirements and Guidelines for Schools on maximum background noise level and reverberation times;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) State Fire Code;</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) chapter 326B governing building codes; and</p>
          <p class="in">(vii) consultation with affected government units about the impact of the project on utilities, roads, sewers, sidewalks, retention ponds, school bus and automobile traffic, access to mass transit, and safe access for pedestrians and cyclists.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.4.28.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 4 s 28</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Review of proposals.</span></h2>
          <p>In reviewing each proposal, the commissioner shall submit to the school board, within 60 days of receiving the proposal, the review and comment about the educational and economic advisability of the project. The commissioner must include comments from residents of the school district in the review and comment. The review and comment shall be based on information submitted with the proposal and other information the commissioner determines is necessary. If the commissioner submits a negative review and comment for a portion of a proposal, the review and comment shall clearly specify which portion of the proposal received a negative review and comment and which portion of the proposal received a positive review and comment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.71.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Publication.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At least 48 days but not more than 70 days before a referendum for bonds under chapter 475 or solicitation of bids for a project that has received a positive or unfavorable review and comment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.70">123B.70</a>, the school board shall publish a summary of the commissioner's review and comment of that project in the legal newspaper of the district. The school board must hold a public meeting to discuss the commissioner's review and comment before such a referendum for bonds. Supplementary information shall be available to the public. Where no such referendum for bonds is required, the publication and public meeting requirements of this subdivision shall not apply.</p>
          <p>(b) The publication requirement in paragraph (a) does not apply to alternative facilities projects approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.595">123B.595</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.71.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.2.15.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 2 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/532/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 532 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/305/#laws.0.45.0">1977 c 305 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.7.6.0">1983 c 314 art 7 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/463/#laws.7.1.0">1984 c 463 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/1/3/#laws.1.16.0">1Sp1986 c 3 art 1 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/258/#laws.0.12.0">1987 c 258 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/718/#laws.8.2.0">1988 c 718 art 8 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/246/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 246 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.5.2.0">1989 c 329 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.5.2.0">1990 c 562 art 5 s 2</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.5.2.0">1991 c 265 art 5 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.13.4.0">1993 c 224 art 13 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.1.2.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 1 s 2</a>; art 16 s 13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.13.5.0">1996 c 412 art 13 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/7/#laws.1.47.0">1997 c 7 art 1 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/231/#laws.16.4.0">1997 c 231 art 16 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/1/4/#laws.4.4.0">1Sp1997 c 4 art 4 s 4</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.4.51.0">1998 c 397 art 4 s 51</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.4.1.0">1998 c 398 art 4 s 1</a>; art 5 s 55; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.5.6.0">2000 c 489 art 5 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.4.7.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 4 s 7</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.4.9.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 4 s 9</a>-11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/140/#laws.4.61.0">2007 c 140 art 4 s 61; art 13 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.4.7.0">2009 c 96 art 4 s 7</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.4.4.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 4 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.18.8.0">2014 c 312 art 18 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.30.15.0">2016 c 189 art 30 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.5.5.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 5 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.1.3.0">2023 c 55 art 1 s 3</a>; art 8 s 11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.8.1.0">2024 c 115 art 8 s 1</a>,2</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 2 was held unconstitutional because its enactment violated the single subject and title requirements of the Minnesota Constitution in <i>Associated Builders and Contractors v. Ventura</i>, 610 N.W.2d 293 (Minn. 2000).</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.72">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.72 SCHOOL FACILITY COMMISSIONING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.72.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to the installation or retrofitting of heating, ventilation, and air conditioning systems for projects where the total project cost per site exceeds $1,400,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.72.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">System inspector.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, system inspector means:</p>
          <p>(1) a Minnesota-licensed architect or engineer; or</p>
          <p>(2) properly qualified testing and balancing agency or individual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.72.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Certification.</span></h2>
          <p>Prior to occupying or reoccupying a school facility affected by this section, a school board or its designee shall submit a document prepared by a system inspector to the building official or to the commissioner, verifying that the facility's heating, ventilation, and air conditioning system has been installed and operates according to design specifications and code, according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71#stat.123B.71.9">123B.71, subdivision 9</a>, clause (7), item (iii). A systems inspector shall also verify that the facility's design will provide the ability for monitoring of outdoor airflow and total airflow of ventilation systems in new school facilities and that any heating, ventilation, or air conditioning system that is installed or modified for a project subject to this section must provide a filtration system with a current ASHRAE standard.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.72.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Occupancy.</span></h2>
          <p>If the document submitted by the school board to the local building official or the commissioner does not demonstrate to that official's satisfaction that the heating, ventilation, and air conditioning system has been installed correctly or that the system is not operating at a level to meet design specifications, the official or commissioner may allow up to one year of occupancy while the heating, ventilation, and air conditioning system is improved to a level that is considered satisfactory by the system inspector.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.72.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/1/4/#laws.4.8.0">1Sp1997 c 4 art 4 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.4.51.0">1998 c 397 art 4 s 51</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.5.7.0">2000 c 489 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.12.6.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 12 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/76/#laws.1.14.0">2011 c 76 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.4.5.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 4 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.18.10.0">2014 c 312 art 18 s 10</a>,11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/85/#laws.0.20.0">2024 c 85 s 20</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.123B.73"><b>123B.73</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/2/#laws.4.32.0">1Sp2003 c 2 art 4 s 32</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.74">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.74 EMINENT DOMAIN.</h1>
        <p>In any municipal corporation or district in this state where the governing body or board has the right, power, and authority to purchase sites for school buildings without authorization by the voters at a regular or special meeting or election called for that purpose, such governing body or school board shall have the right, power, and authority to condemn lands under the power of eminent domain for sites and grounds for public school buildings. The governing body or board shall exercise such power and authority pursuant to the terms and provisions of chapter 117. Any such corporation or district shall have the right, upon the filing of the award of the commissioners provided for in chapter 117, and upon giving the notice therein required of the filing of such award, to enter upon and appropriate the lands so condemned without giving of any bond. In case of such entry and appropriation, such corporation or district shall be bound absolutely to pay all damages awarded, either by the commissioners or by the court upon appeal therefrom, together with all costs and expenses adjudged against it therein within the time specified in chapter 117. In case any such corporation or district shall appeal from the award of commissioners appointed pursuant to any such condemnation proceedings, such corporation or school district shall not be required to give or file any appeal bond therein.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.74.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.4.29.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 4 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.102.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 102</a>,124; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/214/#laws.0.20.0">2006 c 214 s 20</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.744">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.744 AGRICULTURAL EDUCATION.</h1>
        <p>In any district in which instruction in agriculture is afforded, the board is authorized and empowered to purchase or otherwise acquire by condemnation proceedings as provided for acquiring schoolhouse sites in the name and in behalf of such district, a suitable tract of land either within or outside of the limits of such district to be used for the purpose of instruction, experimentation, and demonstration in agriculture. The provisions of this section shall apply as well to districts organized under special acts as under the general laws, notwithstanding any provisions or restrictions in the laws under which the districts are organized.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.744.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.4.30.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 4 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.103.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 103</a>,124</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.747">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.747 NATIONAL FOREST LAND FUNDS, HANDLING AND DISPOSITION.</h1>
        <p>A county board may place the money received by such county from the federal government for and on account of any national forest lands situated in the county into a special fund to be disbursed and paid over to any district maintaining and operating any school wholly or partly within an area constituting a part of any auxiliary or state forest. The board must adopt a resolution to take such action. The resolution must specify the terms and conditions under which the money shall be paid over and disbursed to any district.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.747.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.5.51.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 5 s 51</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.116.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 116</a>,124</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DISTRICT FINANCES</h1>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.123B.749"><b>123B.749</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.5.14.0">2007 c 146 art 5 s 14</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.75">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.75 REVENUE; REPORTING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>District revenues must be recognized and reported on the district books of account in accordance with this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section, "school district tax settlement revenue" means the current, delinquent, and manufactured home property tax receipts collected by the county and distributed to the school district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Applicability to period and fund.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in this section, revenues must be recorded in a manner which clearly indicates that they are applicable to a specific accounting period and fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Receivable.</span></h2>
          <p>Receivables must be recorded in a manner which clearly reflects the amounts of money due to a particular fund from public and private sources at the date of each accounting statement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Recognized as receivable.</span></h2>
          <p>All current levies of local taxes, including portions assumed by the state, shall be recognized as receivable at the beginning of the calendar year during which collection normally takes place.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Taconite revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>Taconite revenue received in a calendar year by a school district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.28">298.28, subdivisions 4, paragraphs (b)</a> and (c), and 11, paragraph (d), is fully recognized in the fiscal year in which the February payment falls.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Levy recognition.</span></h2>
          <p> For fiscal year 2014 and later years, in June of each year, the school district must recognize as revenue, in the fund for which the levy was made, the lesser of:</p>
          <p>(1) the sum of May, June, and July school district tax settlement revenue received in that calendar year, plus general education aid according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.13#stat.126C.13.4">126C.13, subdivision 4</a>, received in July and August of that calendar year; or</p>
          <p>(2) the sum of:</p>
          <p>(i) 31 percent of the referendum levy certified according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17">126C.17</a> in calendar year 2000; plus</p>
          <p>(ii) the entire amount of the levy certified in the prior calendar year according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.4531">124D.4531</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.862">124D.862</a>, for Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, Independent School District No. 625, St. Paul, and Independent School District No. 709, Duluth; 126C.41, subdivisions 1, 2, paragraph (a), and 3, paragraphs (b), (c), and (d); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.43#stat.126C.43.2">126C.43, subdivision 2</a>; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.48">126C.48</a>, subdivision 6.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">State aids or grants as revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>State aids or grants, that are paid as a matching of an expenditure, shall be recognized as revenues and recorded as receivables in the fiscal year during which the eligible expenditure is recognized.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.7.23.0">2013 c 116 art 7 s 23</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.6b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6b.<span class="headnote">General education aid.</span></h2>
          <p>If the amount to be recognized as revenue under subdivision 5 exceeds the May, June, and July school district tax settlement revenue received in that calendar year, the district must recognize an amount of general education aid equal to the difference between the total amount to be recognized as revenue under subdivision 5, and the May, June, and July school district tax settlement revenue received in that calendar year as revenue in the previous fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Other revenues recognized.</span></h2>
          <p>Other revenues not specified in this section shall be recognized as revenue and shall be recorded in the fiscal year earned.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Deviations footnoted.</span></h2>
          <p>Deviations from the principles set forth in this section must be evaluated and explained in footnotes to audited financial statements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Commissioner shall specify fiscal year.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall specify the fiscal year or years to which the revenue from any aid or tax levy is applicable if Minnesota Statutes do not so specify. The commissioner must report to the chairs and ranking minority members of the house of representatives and senate committees with jurisdiction over education finance by January 15 of each year any adjustments under this subdivision in the previous year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.75.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Insurance premium refund.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If money collected by an entity providing group insurance under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.6161#stat.471.6161.1">471.6161, subdivision 1</a>, for the payment of insurance premiums is above the cost of that coverage and returned to the school district purchasing that coverage as a refund, that school district must negotiate with the exclusive representative regarding the refund amount attributable to the proportionate number of insured lives covered by that exclusive representative.</p>
          <p>(b) If there is no exclusive representative or if the employer and the exclusive representative are unable to come to an agreement within 150 days, the remaining refunds shall be used to pay the full premium to the program for any employees not covered by an agreement negotiated under this section until the proportionate refunds are depleted. These refunds shall be used for a proportional premium payment at the time it is necessary to deplete the balance.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.75.history
			">
