      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.76">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.76 EXPENDITURES; REPORTING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.76.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Recognition.</span></h2>
          <p>District expenditures must be recognized and reported on the district books of account in accordance with this section.</p>
          <p>Fiscal year-end recognition of expenditures and the related offsetting liabilities must be recorded in each fund in accordance with the uniform financial accounting and reporting standards for Minnesota school districts. Encumbrances outstanding at the end of the fiscal year do not constitute expenditures or liabilities.</p>
          <p>Deviations from the principles set forth in this subdivision must be evaluated and explained in footnotes to audited financial statements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.76.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Accounting.</span></h2>
          <p>Expenditures for any legal purpose of the district not accounted for elsewhere must be accounted for in the general fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.76.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Expenditures by building.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For the purposes of this section, "building" means education site as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.04#stat.123B.04.1">123B.04, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Each district shall maintain separate accounts to identify general fund expenditures for each building. All expenditures for regular instruction, secondary vocational instruction, and school administration must be reported to the department separately for each building. All expenditures for special education instruction, instructional support services, and pupil support services provided within a specific building must be reported to the department separately for each building. Salary expenditures reported by building must reflect actual salaries for staff at the building and must not be based on districtwide averages. All other general fund expenditures may be reported by building or on a districtwide basis.</p>
          <p>(c) The department must annually report information showing school district general fund expenditures per pupil by program category for each building and estimated school district general fund revenue generated by pupils attending each building on its website. For purposes of this report:</p>
          <p>(1) expenditures not reported by building shall be allocated among buildings on a uniform per pupil basis;</p>
          <p>(2) basic skills revenue shall be allocated according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.4">126C.10, subdivision 4</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) secondary sparsity revenue and elementary sparsity revenue shall be allocated according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10">126C.10</a>, subdivisions 7 and 8;</p>
          <p>(4) alternative teacher compensation revenue shall be allocated according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.415">122A.415</a>, subdivision 1;</p>
          <p>(5) other general education revenue shall be allocated on a uniform per pupil unit basis;</p>
          <p>(6) state and federal special education aid and Title I aid shall be allocated in proportion to district expenditures for these programs by building; and</p>
          <p>(7) other general fund revenues shall be allocated on a uniform per pupil basis, except that the department may allocate other revenues attributable to specific buildings directly to those buildings.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.76.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.29.0">1976 c 271 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.7.4.0">1980 c 609 art 7 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.7.8.0">1981 c 358 art 7 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.12.12.0">1993 c 224 art 12 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.1.2.0">1996 c 412 art 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.17.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 17</a>,124; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/294/#laws.1.3.0">2004 c 294 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.4.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/263/#laws.1.3.0">2006 c 263 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/76/#laws.1.15.0">2011 c 76 art 1 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.77">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.77 ACCOUNTING, BUDGETING, AND REPORTING REQUIREMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.77.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Uniform financial accounting and reporting standards.</span></h2>
          <p>Each Minnesota school district must adopt the uniform financial accounting and reporting standards for Minnesota school districts provided for in guidelines adopted by the department.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.77.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">School district consolidated financial statement.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner, in consultation with the advisory committee on financial management, accounting, and reporting, shall develop and maintain a school district consolidated financial statement format that converts uniform financial accounting and reporting standards data under subdivision 1 into a more understandable format.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.77.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Audited financial statement.</span></h2>
          <p>Each district must submit to the commissioner by September 15 of each year unaudited financial data for the preceding fiscal year. These financial data must be submitted in the format prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.77.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Statement for comparison and correction.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) By November 30 of the calendar year of the submission of the unaudited financial data, the district must provide to the commissioner audited financial data for the preceding fiscal year. The audit must be conducted in compliance with generally accepted governmental auditing standards, the federal Single Audit Act, and the Minnesota legal compliance guide issued by the Office of the State Auditor. An audited financial statement prepared in a form which will allow comparison with and correction of material differences in the unaudited financial data shall be submitted to the commissioner and the state auditor by December 31. The audited financial statement must also provide a statement of assurance pertaining to uniform financial accounting and reporting standards compliance and a copy of the management letter submitted to the district by the school district's auditor.</p>
          <p>(b) By February 1 of the calendar year following the submission of the unaudited financial data, the commissioner shall convert the audited financial data required by this subdivision into the consolidated financial statement format required under subdivision 1a and publish the information on the department's website.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.77.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Budget approval.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Prior to July 1 of each year, the board of each district must approve and adopt its revenue and expenditure budgets for the next school year. The budget document so adopted must be considered an expenditure-authorizing or appropriations document. No funds shall be expended by any board or district for any purpose in any school year prior to the adoption of the budget document which authorizes that expenditure, or prior to an amendment to the budget document by the board to authorize the expenditure. Expenditures of funds in violation of this subdivision shall be considered unlawful expenditures.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.77.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Joint powers agreements; service cooperatives, education districts.</span></h2>
          <p>All governmental units formed by joint powers agreements entered into by districts pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.22">123A.22</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.26">125A.26</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.48">125A.48</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a>, or any other law and all service cooperatives and education districts are subject to the provisions of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.77.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>A district providing early retirement incentive payments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.48">122A.48</a>, severance pay under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/465.72">465.72</a>, or health insurance benefits to retired employees under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.61">471.61</a>, must account for the payments according to uniform financial accounting and reporting standards.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.77.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.30.0">1976 c 271 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/447/#laws.7.7.0">1977 c 447 art 7 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.7.5.0">1980 c 609 art 7 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.4.5.0">1982 c 548 art 4 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.7.7.0">1983 c 314 art 7 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/463/#laws.7.3.0">1984 c 463 art 7 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.6.3.0">1989 c 329 art 6 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.8.6.0">1990 c 562 art 8 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.12.13.0">1993 c 224 art 12 s 13</a>,14; art 14 s 5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/647/#laws.13.1.0">1994 c 647 art 13 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/305/#laws.1.138.0">1996 c 305 art 1 s 138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.18.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 18</a>,124; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.1.2.0">1998 c 398 art 1 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.9.20.0">1999 c 241 art 9 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.6.9.0">2000 c 489 art 6 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/294/#laws.1.4.0">2004 c 294 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/263/#laws.2.8.0">2006 c 263 art 2 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.5.2.0">2007 c 146 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.1.6.0">2009 c 96 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/3/#laws.7.2.0">1Sp2015 c 3 art 7 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.78">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.78 CASH FLOW; SCHOOL DISTRICT REVENUES; BORROWING FOR CURRENT OPERATING COSTS; CAPITAL EXPENDITURE DEFICITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.78.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">State aids payment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of management and budget shall remit all payments of state aids to districts in conformance with the dates provided by law or, when not so provided, with a schedule of aid payments to be established by the commissioner of education in consultation with other affected state agencies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.78.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Taxes.</span></h2>
          <p>The auditors or finance officers of Minnesota counties shall remit all payments of taxes to the districts in conformance with the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/276.11">276.11</a>. Districts which have need for tax remittance advances may secure them from the counties by making formal requests in conformance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/276.11">276.11</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.78.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Tax and aid anticipation certificates.</span></h2>
          <p>Minnesota school districts may issue tax and aid anticipation certificates in conformance with the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.50">126C.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.56">126C.56</a>, with the additional provision that the proceeds of such borrowing or any other method of borrowing shall be recorded as liabilities of funds for which the taxes were levied, or for which the aids are receivable. Nothing in this subdivision provides authority for borrowing against the tax levies and aids of one district fund for the purpose of increasing the available cash balance of another fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.78.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Borrowing for outstanding bonds.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless otherwise provided by law, a district must not, for the purpose of increasing the available cash balance of another fund, borrow or transfer funds from the building construction fund, debt redemption fund, trust and agency fund, or from any sinking fund for outstanding bonds issued for any purpose. However, if the contemplated use for which funds were originally placed in the building construction fund or a sinking fund is afterwards abandoned or if a balance remains after the use is accomplished, a district may devote these funds as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/475.65">475.65</a>. For the purpose of insuring fund integrity, on determining that a district is in violation of this subdivision or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.75">123B.75</a>, the commissioner shall require that such district maintain separate bank accounts for building construction funds, debt redemption funds, trust and agency funds, and sinking funds for outstanding bonds. Nothing in this subdivision shall be construed to prohibit the use of common bank accounts for other funds unless prohibited by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.78.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Deficit for capital projects.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon approval by the commissioner, a district may incur a deficit in the reserve for operating capital account for a period not to exceed three years to provide money for capital projects. The commissioner shall approve a description of the project and a financial plan to recover the deficit prior to the initiation of the project.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.78.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.31.0">1976 c 271 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.6.2.0">1983 c 314 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.2.1.0">1987 c 384 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.1.3.0">1996 c 412 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.19.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 19</a>,124; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.79">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.79 PERMANENT FUND TRANSFERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Limitations.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in this subdivision, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.51">123B.51</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.80">123B.80</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/475.61">475.61</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/475.65">475.65</a>, a district may not permanently transfer money from (1) an operating fund to a nonoperating fund; (2) a nonoperating fund to another nonoperating fund; or (3) a nonoperating fund to an operating fund. Permanent transfers may be made from any fund to any other fund to correct for prior fiscal years' errors discovered after the books have been closed for that year. Permanent transfers may be made from the general fund to any other operating funds according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142D.093">142D.093</a> or if the resources of the other fund are not adequate to finance approved expenditures from that other fund. Permanent transfers may also be made from the general fund to eliminate deficits in another fund when that other fund is being discontinued. When a district discontinues operation of a district-owned bus fleet or a substantial portion of a fleet, the balance shall cancel to the district's general fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.30.26.0">2016 c 189 art 30 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">TRA and FICA transfer.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 1, a district may transfer money from the general fund to the community service fund for the employer contributions for teacher retirement and FICA for employees who are members of a teacher retirement association and who are paid from the community service fund.</p>
          <p>(b) A district must not transfer money under paragraph (a) for employees who are paid with money other than normal operating funds, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.27">354.05, subdivision 27</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Operating fund; nonoperating fund; defined.</span></h2>
          <p>As used in this section, "operating fund" and "nonoperating fund" have the meanings specified in the uniform financial accounting and reporting standards for Minnesota school districts. Any transfer for a period in excess of one year shall be deemed to be a permanent transfer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Deficits; exception.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section, a permanent transfer includes creating a deficit in a nonoperating fund for a period past the end of the current fiscal year which is covered by moneys in an operating fund. A deficit in the reserve for operating capital account pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.78#stat.123B.78.5">123B.78, subdivision 5</a>, does not constitute a permanent transfer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.30.26.0">2016 c 189 art 30 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Account transfer for designated separation and retirement benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may separately maintain in a designated for separation and retirement benefit account the amount necessary to meet the obligations for separation and retirement benefits, including compensated absences, termination benefits, pension benefits, and other postemployment benefits, not accounted for elsewhere. The amount necessary must be calculated according to standards established by the department.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Account transfer for reorganizing districts.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A district that has reorganized according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.35">123A.35</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.43">123A.43</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.46">123A.46</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.48">123A.48</a>, or has conducted a successful referendum on the question of combination under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.37#stat.123A.37.2">123A.37, subdivision 2</a>, or consolidation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.48#stat.123A.48.15">123A.48, subdivision 15</a>, or has been assigned an identification number by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.48#stat.123A.48.16">123A.48, subdivision 16</a>, may make permanent transfers between any of the funds or accounts in the newly created or enlarged district with the exception of the debt redemption fund, building construction fund, food service fund, and long-term facilities maintenance account of the general fund. Fund transfers under this section may be made for up to one year prior to the effective date of combination or consolidation by the consolidating boards and during the year following the effective date of reorganization by the consolidated board. The newly formed board of the combined district may adopt a resolution on or before August 30 of the year of the reorganization authorizing a transfer among accounts or funds of the previous independent school districts which transfer or transfers shall be reported in the affected districts' audited financial statements for the year immediately preceding the consolidation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.79.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Elimination of reserve accounts.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Any balance in the district's reserved account for deferred maintenance as of June 30, 2016, shall be transferred to the reserved account for long-term facilities maintenance in the school district's general fund. Any balance in the district's reserved account for health and safety as of June 30, 2019, shall be transferred to the unassigned fund balance account in the district's general fund. Any balance in the district's reserved account for alternative facilities as of June 30, 2016, shall be transferred to the reserved account for long-term facilities maintenance in the district's building construction fund.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.79.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>

      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.80">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.80 EXCEPTIONS FOR PERMANENT FUND TRANSFERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.80.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Commissioner's authorization.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may authorize a board to transfer money from any fund or account to another fund or account according to this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.80.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>A board requesting authority to transfer money must apply to the commissioner and provide information requested. The application must indicate the law or rule prohibiting the desired transfer. The application must be signed by the superintendent and approved by the school board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.80.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Approval standard.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may approve a request only when an event has occurred in a district that could not have been foreseen by the district. The event must relate directly to the fund or account involved and to the amount to be transferred.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.80.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/12/#laws.7.8.0">1Sp1985 c 12 art 7 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/143/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 143 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.13.7.0">1993 c 224 art 13 s 7</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.27.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 27</a>,28,124; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.5.1.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 5 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.81">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.81 STATUTORY OPERATING DEBT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Operating debt.</span></h2>
          <p>The "operating debt" of a school district means the net negative unreserved general fund balance calculated as of June 30 of each year in accordance with the uniform financial accounting and reporting standards for Minnesota school districts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Statutory operating debt.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">If the amount of the operating debt is more than 2-1/2 percent of the most recent fiscal year's expenditure amount for the funds considered under subdivision 1, the net negative undesignated fund balance is defined as "statutory operating debt" for the purposes of this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.83">123B.83</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Debt verification.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall establish a uniform auditing or other verification procedure for districts to determine whether a statutory operating debt exists in any Minnesota school district. The standards for this uniform auditing or verification procedure must be promulgated by the commissioner. If a district applies to the commissioner for a statutory operating debt verification, the commissioner shall require a verification of the amount of the statutory operating debt which actually does exist.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Debt elimination.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">If an audit or other verification procedure conducted pursuant to subdivision 3 determines that a statutory operating debt exists, a district must follow the procedures in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.83">123B.83 </a>to eliminate this statutory operating debt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Certification of debt.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall certify the amount of statutory operating debt for each district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.54.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">This section is applicable only to common, independent, and special school districts and districts formed pursuant to Laws 1967, chapter 822, as amended, and Laws 1969, chapters 775 and 1060, as amended. This section does not apply to Independent School District No. 625.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.81.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.11.19.0">2007 c 146 art 11 s 19</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.81.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.32.0">1976 c 271 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/447/#laws.7.9.0">1977 c 447 art 7 s 9</a>-12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.7.7.0">1980 c 609 art 7 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/424/#laws.0.130.0">1982 c 424 s 130</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/398/#laws.7.42.0">1987 c 398 art 7 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.37.0">1991 c 130 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.12.29.0">1992 c 499 art 12 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.13.6.0">1996 c 412 art 13 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.29.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 29</a>-35,124; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.6.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.11.4.0">2007 c 146 art 11 s 4</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.5.4.0">2009 c 96 art 5 s 4</a>-6</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.82">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.82 REORGANIZATION OPERATING DEBT.</h1>
        <p>The "reorganization operating debt" of a school district means the net negative unreserved fund balances in all school district funds, other than building construction, debt redemption, and trust and agency, calculated in accordance with the uniform financial accounting and reporting standards for Minnesota school districts as of:</p>
        <p>(1) June 30 of the fiscal year before the first year that a district receives revenue according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.39#stat.123A.39.3">123A.39, subdivision 3</a>; or</p>
        <p>(2) June 30 of the fiscal year before the effective date of reorganization according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.46">123A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.48">123A.48</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.82.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.6.3.0">1991 c 265 art 6 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.13.7.0">1996 c 412 art 13 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.124.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 124</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/294/#laws.1.5.0">2004 c 294 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.7.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.83">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.83 EXPENDITURE LIMITATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.83.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 55</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.83.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Net unreserved general fund balances.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A school district must limit its expenditures so that its net unreserved general fund balance does not constitute statutory operating debt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.81">123B.81</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.83.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Failure to limit expenditures.</span></h2>
          <p>If a district does not limit its expenditures in accordance with this section, the commissioner may so notify the appropriate committees of the legislature by no later than February 15 of the year following the end of that fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.83.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Special operating plan.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the net negative unreserved general fund balance, calculated in accordance with the uniform financial accounting and reporting standards for Minnesota school districts, as of June 30 each year, is more than 2-1/2 percent of the year's expenditure amount, the district must, prior to January 31 of the next fiscal year, submit a special operating plan to reduce the district's deficit expenditures to the commissioner for approval. The commissioner may also require the district to provide evidence that the district meets and will continue to meet all high school graduation requirements.</p>
          <p>Notwithstanding any other law to the contrary, a district submitting a special operating plan to the commissioner under this clause which is disapproved by the commissioner must not receive any aid pursuant to chapters 120B, 122A, 123A, 123B, 124D, 125A, 126C, and 127A until a special operating plan of the district is so approved.</p>
          <p>(b) A district must receive aids pending the approval of its special operating plan under paragraph (a). A district which complies with its approved operating plan must receive aids as long as the district continues to comply with the approved operating plan.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.83.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.33.0">1976 c 271 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/447/#laws.7.13.0">1977 c 447 art 7 s 13</a>,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/334/#laws.6.5.0">1979 c 334 art 6 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.7.8.0">1980 c 609 art 7 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.7.11.0">1981 c 358 art 7 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/398/#laws.7.42.0">1987 c 398 art 7 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.8.8.0">1990 c 562 art 8 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.37.0">1991 c 130 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.11.7.0">1991 c 265 art 11 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.12.29.0">1992 c 499 art 12 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.1.4.0">1996 c 412 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.6.36.0">1998 c 397 art 6 s 36</a>,124; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.9.21.0">1999 c 241 art 9 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.6.11.0">2000 c 489 art 6 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.8.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.11.7.0">2007 c 146 art 11 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.1.7.0">2009 c 96 art 1 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">EQUAL TREATMENT IN TRANSPORTING STUDENTS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.84">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.84 POLICY.</h1>
        <p>In districts where the state provides aids for transportation it is in the public interest to provide equality of treatment in transporting school children of the state who are required to attend elementary and secondary schools pursuant to chapter 120A, so that the health, welfare and safety of the children, while using the public highways of the state, shall be protected.</p>
        <p>School children attending any schools, complying with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22">120A.22</a>, are therefore entitled to the same rights and privileges relating to transportation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.84.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/570/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 570 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/209/#laws.2.1.0">1989 c 209 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.1.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 1</a>,164; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.85">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.85 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.85.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>The following words and terms in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/121A.585">121A.585</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/121A.59">121A.59</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.84">123B.84</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.87">123B.87</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.90">123B.90</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.91">123B.91</a>, shall have the following meanings ascribed to them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.85.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">District.</span></h2>
          <p>"District" means any school district as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.05">120A.05</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.85.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">School.</span></h2>
          <p>"School" means any school as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22#stat.120A.22.4">120A.22, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.85.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">School board.</span></h2>
          <p>"School board" means the governing body of any school district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.85.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">School children.</span></h2>
          <p>"School children" means any student or child attending or required to attend any school as provided in the Education Code, chapters 120A to 129C.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.85.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/570/#laws.0.2.0">1969 c 570 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/162/#laws.0.41.0">1975 c 162 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/209/#laws.2.1.0">1989 c 209 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/186/#laws.0.119.0">1995 c 186 s 119</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.164.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 164</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.10.3.0">2000 c 489 art 10 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.86">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.86 EQUAL TREATMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.86.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General provisions.</span></h2>
          <p>A district shall provide equal transportation within the district for all school children to any school when transportation is deemed necessary by the school board because of distance or traffic condition in like manner and form as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.88">123B.88</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92">123B.92</a>, when applicable.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.86.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Nonpublic school students.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board of any local district must provide school bus transportation to the district boundary for school children residing in the district at least the same distance from a nonpublic school actually attended in another district as public school pupils are transported in the transporting district. Such transportation must be provided whether or not there is another nonpublic school within the transporting district, if the transportation is to schools maintaining grades or departments not maintained in the district or if the attendance of such children at school can more safely, economically, or conveniently be provided for by such means.</p>
          <p>(b) The school board of any local district may provide school bus transportation to a nonpublic school in another district for school children residing in the district and attending that school, whether or not there is another nonpublic school within the transporting district, if the transportation is to schools maintaining grades or departments not maintained in the district or if the attendance of such children at school can more safely, economically, or conveniently be provided for by such means. If the board transports children to a nonpublic school located in another district, the nonpublic school must pay the cost of such transportation provided outside the district boundaries.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.86.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Board control.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) When transportation is provided, the scheduling of routes, manner and method of transportation, control and discipline of school children and any other matter relating thereto shall be within the sole discretion, control and management of the board.</p>
          <p>(b) A school board and a nonpublic school may mutually agree to a written plan for the board to provide nonpublic pupil transportation to nonpublic school students.</p>
          <p>(c) A school board that provides pupil transportation through the school's employees may transport nonpublic school students according to the plan and retain the nonpublic pupil transportation aid attributable to that plan. A nonpublic school may make a payment to the school district to cover additional transportation services agreed to in the written plan for nonpublic pupil transportation services not required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.84">123B.84</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.87">123B.87</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) A school board that contracts for pupil transportation services may enter into a contractual arrangement with a school bus contractor according to the written plan adopted by the school board and the nonpublic school to transport nonpublic school students and retain the nonpublic pupil transportation aid attributable to that plan for the purposes of paying the school bus contractor. A nonpublic school may make a payment to the school district to cover additional transportation services agreed to in the written plan for nonpublic pupil transportation services included in the contract that are not required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.84">123B.84</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.87">123B.87</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) The school district must report the number of nonpublic school students transported and the nonpublic pupil transportation expenditures incurred under paragraph (b) in the form and manner specified by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.86.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Rules.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of education may amend rules relating to equal transportation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.86.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/570/#laws.0.3.0">1969 c 570 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/566/#laws.0.1.0">1974 c 566 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/51/#laws.0.1.0">1975 c 51 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/162/#laws.0.27.0">1975 c 162 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/2/#laws.0.58.0">1976 c 2 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/424/#laws.0.31.0">1982 c 424 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.2.1.0">1982 c 548 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/655/#laws.1.24.0">1984 c 655 art 1 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/647/#laws.12.2.0">1994 c 647 art 12 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.2.4.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.2.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 2</a>,3,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.55.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/254/#laws.0.12.0">2000 c 254 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.1.4.0">2023 c 55 art 1 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.87">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.87 FUNDS AND AIDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.87.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">State aid.</span></h2>
          <p>State aids made available or appropriated shall be for the equal benefit of all school children, and be disbursed in such manner as determined by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.87.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Boards may expend money.</span></h2>
          <p>The board of any district may expend any moneys in its treasury, whether received from state or any other source for the purpose of providing equal transportation treatment of all school children attending school.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.87.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/570/#laws.0.4.0">1969 c 570 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/706/#laws.0.28.0">1978 c 706 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/334/#laws.2.4.0">1979 c 334 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.2.5.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.4.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 4</a>,164</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">SCHOOL BUSES; STUDENT TRANSPORTATION</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123B.88">
        <h1 class="shn">123B.88 INDEPENDENT SCHOOL DISTRICTS; TRANSPORTATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Providing transportation.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may provide for the transportation of pupils to and from school and for any other purpose. The board may also provide for the transportation of pupils to schools in other districts for grades and departments not maintained in the district, including high school, at the expense of the district, when funds are available therefor and if agreeable to the district to which it is proposed to transport the pupils, for the whole or a part of the school year, as it may deem advisable, and subject to its rules. In any district, the board must arrange for the attendance of all pupils living two miles or more from the school, except pupils whose transportation privileges have been voluntarily surrendered under subdivision 2, or whose privileges have been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.91#stat.123B.91.1">123B.91, subdivision 1</a>, clause (5). The district may provide for the transportation of or the boarding and rooming of the pupils who may be more economically and conveniently provided for by that means. Arrangements for attendance may include a requirement that parents or guardians request transportation before it is provided. The board must provide necessary transportation consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (4), for a child with a disability not yet enrolled in kindergarten for the provision of special instruction and services under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.26">125A.26</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.48">125A.48</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a>. Special instruction and services for a child with a disability not yet enrolled in kindergarten include an individualized education program team placement in an early childhood program when that placement is necessary to address the child's level of functioning and needs. When transportation is provided, scheduling of routes, establishment of the location of bus stops, manner and method of transportation, control and discipline of school children, the determination of fees, and any other matter relating thereto must be within the sole discretion, control, and management of the board. The district may provide for the transportation of pupils or expend a reasonable amount for room and board of pupils whose attendance at school can more economically and conveniently be provided for by that means or who attend school in a building rented or leased by a district within the confines of an adjacent district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Full-service school zones.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may establish a full-service school zone by adopting a written resolution and may provide transportation for students attending a school in that full-service school zone. A full-service school zone may be established for a school that is located in an area with higher than average crime or other social and economic challenges and that provides education, health or human services, or other parental support in collaboration with a city, county, state, or nonprofit agency. The pupil transportation must be intended to stabilize enrollment and reduce mobility at the school located in a full-service school zone.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Voluntary surrender of transportation privileges.</span></h2>
          <p>The parent or guardian of a student may voluntarily surrender the student's to and from school transportation privileges granted under subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Transportation services contracts.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may contract for the furnishing of authorized transportation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.52">123B.52</a>, and may purchase gasoline and furnish same to a contract carrier for use in the performance of a contract with the school district for transportation of school children to and from school.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Pupil transportation safety committee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A school board may establish a pupil transportation safety committee. The chair of the pupil transportation safety committee is the district's school transportation safety director. The school board shall appoint the other members of the pupil transportation safety committee. Membership may include parents, school bus drivers, representatives of school bus companies, local law enforcement officials, other school district staff, and representatives from other units of local government.</p>
          <p>(b) The duties of the pupil transportation safety committee include: (1) reviewing and recommending changes to the district's pupil transportation safety policy required under subdivision 1; and (2) developing a comprehensive plan for the safe transportation of students who face hazardous transportation conditions. The comprehensive hazardous transportation plan shall consider safety factors including the types of roads that students must cross, the speed of traffic on those roads, the age of the students, and any other factors as determined by the committee.</p>
          <p>(c) The pupil transportation safety committee must hold at least one public meeting before adopting its comprehensive plan for transporting students who face hazardous transportation conditions.</p>
          <p>(d) Any recommended changes to the district's pupil transportation safety policy and the comprehensive plan for hazardous transportation must be submitted to the school board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Instruction in a nonresident district.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may provide for the instruction of any resident pupil in another district when inadequate room, distance to school, unfavorable road conditions, or other facts or conditions make attendance in the pupil's own district unreasonably difficult or impractical, in which case such district shall pay to the district so attended the tuition agreed upon or charged, pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.488#stat.123A.488.2">123A.488, subdivision 2</a>, and may provide transportation; provided, that such pupil shall continue to be a pupil of the district of residence for the payment of apportionment and other state aids.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Admission of nonresident pupils.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may provide for the admission to the schools of the district, of nonresident pupils, and those above school age, and fix the rates of tuition for such pupils.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Nonresident pupil defined.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this subdivision, a "nonresident pupil" is a pupil who resides in one district, defined as the "resident district" and attends school in another district, defined as the "nonresident district."</p>
          <p>If requested, a nonresident district shall transport a nonresident pupil within its borders and may transport a nonresident pupil within the pupil's resident district. If a nonresident district decides to transport a nonresident pupil within the pupil's resident district, the nonresident district must notify the pupil's resident district of its decision, in writing, prior to providing transportation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Attendance in another state.</span></h2>
          <p>If high school pupils from a district within this state are being transported to a school in another state, the board of the district from which the pupils are being transported may provide free transportation and tuition for any or all of its elementary pupils to such school in another state and be entitled to state aid as provided by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Authority to rent buses.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may rent a bus owned by the district excluding a motor-coach bus to any person for any lawful purpose. Bus rental must not interfere with the transportation of pupils by the district. A lessee may use and operate the bus without payment of a motor vehicle tax. The lessee is liable for any claims for injuries and damages arising out of the use and operation of a bus leased from the district. Except as provided in subdivision 15, the lessee shall procure insurance at the lessee's expense protecting the board and the district against claims for injuries and damages arising out of the use and operation of the bus.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Nonpupil transportation; insurance.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/221.021">221.021</a>, any public school district or school bus contractor providing transportation services to a district on a regular basis in this state may operate school buses, excluding motor coach buses, for the purpose of providing transportation to nonpupils of the school district attending school events, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.49#stat.123B.49.3">123B.49, subdivision 3</a> or 4, provided that no carrier having a charter carrier permit has its principal office and place of business or bus garage within 12 miles of the principal office of the district. District owned buses and the operators thereof shall otherwise comply with the provisions of this section and the rules of the commissioner of public safety and shall be insured in at least the amounts stated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/466.04#stat.466.04.1">466.04, subdivision 1</a>. In all cases the total cost of providing such services, as determined by sound accounting procedures, shall be paid by charges made against those using the buses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Transportation of any person.</span></h2>
          <p>Districts may use district owned or contractor operated school buses to provide transportation along regular school bus routes on a space available basis for any person. Such use of a bus must not interfere with the transportation of pupils to and from school or other authorized transportation of pupils. In all cases, the total additional cost of providing these services, as determined by sound accounting procedures, must be paid by charges made against those using these services or some third-party payor. In no case shall the additional cost of this transportation be paid by the district.</p>
          <p>The provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/65B.47#stat.65B.47.4">65B.47, subdivision 4</a>, shall be applicable to any person being transported pursuant to this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Part-time secondary students.</span></h2>
          <p>Districts may provide bus transportation along regular school bus routes on a space available basis for part-time students enrolled in secondary classes pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.02">124D.02, subdivisions 2, 3, and 4</a>. Such use of a bus must not interfere with the transportation of pupils to and from school or other authorized transportation of pupils. The total additional cost of providing these services, as determined by sound accounting procedures, shall be paid by charges made against those using the services or some third-party payor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Early childhood family education participants.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Districts may provide bus transportation along school bus routes when space is available for participants in early childhood family education programs and school readiness programs if these services do not result in an increase in the district's expenditures for transportation. The costs allocated to these services, as determined by generally accepted accounting principles, shall be considered part of the authorized cost for transportation for the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92">123B.92</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Area learning center pupils between buildings.</span></h2>
          <p>Districts may provide between-building bus transportation along school bus routes when space is available, for pupils attending programs at an area learning center. The transportation is permitted between schools if it does not increase the district's expenditures for transportation. The cost of these services shall be considered part of the authorized cost for the purpose of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92">123B.92</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Transportation insurance.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may provide for the protection of pupils transported for school purposes or activities in district owned, operated, leased, or controlled motor vehicles against injuries or damages arising out of the operation of these vehicles. The board may purchase and pay for insurance from any funds available. An insurance contract covering this risk shall contain a waiver of the defense of governmental immunity. The payment of any insurance premiums by the district does not in itself make the district liable for any injuries or damages incurred by the transportation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Insurance; indemnity.</span></h2>
          <p>If a school board has obtained insurance pursuant to subdivision 14 or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/466.06">466.06</a>, it may also obtain and pay for insurance coverage to indemnify a lessee and to protect the board and the district, in any amount not exceeding the limits of coverage provided for the insurance obtained pursuant to subdivision 14 or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/466.06">466.06</a> against claims for injuries and damages arising out of the use and operation of a district-owned bus while it is leased or rented to the lessee pursuant to subdivision 8. The rental charge shall include the cost of this additional insurance coverage. The procurement of this additional insurance coverage constitutes a waiver of the defense of governmental immunity to the extent of the additional coverage but has no effect on the liability of the board, the district, or its employees beyond the coverage so provided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Payment of insurance premiums; nonliability.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may provide and pay the premiums for the protection for school children, instructors and automobile owners, and any other agency cooperating in providing cars for districts where driver training courses are being offered, against public liability, property damage, collision, fire and theft, arising out of the operation of any vehicle used in the courses. Nothing herein shall make the district liable for injuries resulting from the actions of such persons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Insurance; school safety patrol.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may provide and pay the premiums for insurance against injuries resulting to its pupils while assigned to and acting on a school safety patrol. Such insurance may provide for the payment of either cash benefits to such injured pupil or for the payment of hospital and medical benefits to or for such injured pupil, or both. Nothing herein shall be construed to make the district liable for such injuries.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Snow removal.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may enter into contracts for the removal of snow from roads used for regular bus routes transporting pupils to and from school either within or outside the district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Disabled person transport to day training and habilitation program.</span></h2>
          <p>The board must contract with any licensed day training and habilitation program attended by a resident disabled person who fulfills the eligibility requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.092">256B.092</a>, to transport the resident disabled person to the program in return for payment by the program of the cost of the transportation, if transportation by the board is in the best interest of the disabled person and is not unreasonably burdensome to the district and if a less expensive, reasonable, alternative means of transporting the disabled person does not exist. If the board and the program are unable to agree to a contract, either the board or the program may appeal to the commissioner to resolve the conflict. All decisions of the commissioner shall be final and binding upon the board and the program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Custodial parent transportation.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may provide transportation for a pupil who is a custodial parent and that pupil's child between the pupil's home and a child care provider and between the provider and the school. The board must establish criteria for transportation it provides according to this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Pupil transport on staff development days.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may provide bus transportation between home and school for pupils on days devoted to parent-teacher conferences, teacher's workshops, or other staff development opportunities. If approved by the commissioner as part of a program of educational improvement, the cost of providing this transportation, as determined by generally accepted accounting principles, must be considered part of the authorized cost for regular transportation for the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92">123B.92</a>. The commissioner shall approve inclusion of these costs in the regular transportation category only if the total number of instructional hours in the school year divided by the total number of days for which transportation is provided equals or exceeds the number of instructional hours per day prescribed in the rules of the Department of Education.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123B.88.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Postsecondary enrollment options pupils.</span></h2>
          <p>Districts may provide bus transportation along school bus routes when space is available, for pupils attending programs at a postsecondary institution under the postsecondary enrollment options program. Fees collected for this service under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.36#stat.123B.36.1">123B.36, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (13), shall be subtracted from the authorized cost for nonregular transportation for the purpose of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92">123B.92</a>. A school district may provide transportation for a pupil participating in an articulated program operated under an agreement between the school district and the postsecondary institution.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123B.88.history
			">
